
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- CBR-CDB ---
provide me the number of patients who have been diagnosed with chordae tendinae rupture.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."LONG_TITLE" = "Rupture of chordae tendineae"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."long_title"', '=', 'rupture of chordae tendineae']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."SHORT_TITLE" = "Chordae tendinae rupture"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."short_title"', '=', 'chordae tendinae rupture']]}
----------

Calulate the total number of patients who had icd9 code 5771
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN DIAGNOSES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = DIAGNOSES.HADM_ID WHERE DIAGNOSES."ICD9_CODE" = "5771"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'diagnoses'], 'cond': [['diagnoses."icd9_code"', '=', '5771']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."ICD9_CODE" = "5771"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."icd9_code"', '=', '5771']]}
----------

How many married patients died in or before 2165?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."EXPIRE_FLAG" = "1" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2165.0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2165.0'], ['demographic."expire_flag"', '=', '1'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'married']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DOD_YEAR" <= "2165.0"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."dod_year"', '<=', '2165.0'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'married']]}

give me the number of patients less than 41 years who have delta abnormal lab test status.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "41" AND LAB."FLAG" = "abnormal"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '41'], ['lab."flag"', '=', 'abnormal']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."AGE" < "41" AND LAB."FLAG" = "delta"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."age"', '<', '41'], ['lab."flag"', '=', 'delta']]}
----------
